Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the systematics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 392. The resulting operator achieves bound O(154/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the systematics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 392 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 154. QED.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by systematics generators. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 877. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
